# Third-party notices

hushrun is built with the open-source Rust crates below, for `aarch64-apple-darwin`, `x86_64-apple-darwin`, `aarch64-unknown-linux-musl`, `x86_64-unknown-linux-musl`, `aarch64-pc-windows-msvc` and `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc`. Each crate is under its own license, the one its Cargo.toml declares, and its source is on crates.io at the version listed.

The license texts follow the list. Each is given once, with the crates whose packages hold it.

This file is generated from Cargo.lock and the crates' own license files; don't edit it by hand.

### Text 130

ring 0.17.14 (`LICENSE`).

```text
*ring* uses an "ISC" license, like BoringSSL used to use, for new code
files. See LICENSE-other-bits for the text of that license.

See LICENSE-BoringSSL for code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the
Apache 2.0 license. Some code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the ISC
license. In each case, the license info is at the top of the file.

See src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-APACHE and src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-MIT
for the license to code that was sourced from the once_cell project.
```
